theorem PIDRealTail.sharp : ∀ {x : ℝ} (n : ℕ),
  0 ≤ x →
    x < 1 →
      0 ≤ PIDRealTail.halfTail n x ∧
        PIDRealTail.halfTail n x ≤ PIDRealTail.upper n x ∧
          (0 < x → 0 < PIDRealTail.halfTail n x ∧ PIDRealTail.halfTail n x < PIDRealTail.upper n x) :=
fun {x} n h₀ h₁ =>
  have h_lower := fun y h_a h_b =>
    have h_next := Real.sum_range_le_log_div (LT.lt.le h_a) h_b (n + 1);
    have h_term :=
      div_pos (pow_pos h_a (2 * n + 1))
        (Right.add_pos_of_nonneg_of_pos
          (mul_nonneg
            (le_of_lt
              (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.pos_of_isNat (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ (Eq.refl 2))
                (Eq.refl (Nat.ble 1 2))))

          (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.pos_of_isNat (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one)
            (Eq.refl (Nat.ble 1 1))));

            (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)

                              (zero_div (2 * ↑n + 1))))

                      (and_self False)))
                  imp_self._simp_1))

          (and_self True)))
